6a1('bum): @#//zad pa'am/spang ba'am/skye ba 'am 'gag par 'gyur ram/so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi'i de bzhin nyid gang yin pa de zad pa 'am/spang ba'am/

6b1('bum): gshegs pa de ni ma lags so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor de ltar slob pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po ni/de bzhin nyid la slob pa ste/rnam pa 
6b2('bum): thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la slob pa yin/de bzhin nyid ni zad pa 'am/spang ba 'am/skye ba 'am/'gag par 'gyur ba med de/rab 'byor de ltar slob ba'i byang chub sems dpa' 
6b3('bum): sems dpa' chen po ni//de bzhin nyid la slob pa de/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du slob pa yin/rab 'byor de ltar slobapa'i byang chub sems dpa'/sems dpa' chen po ni/sbyin pa'i

6b6('bum): po ni/nang stong pa nyid la slob pa yin rab 'byor de ltar slob pa'i byang chub sems dba' sems dpa' chen po ni/phyi stong pa nyid la slob pa yan/rab 'byor de ltar slo ba pa'i
7a1('bum): @#//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po ni/phyi nang stong pa nyid la slob pa yin/rab 'byor de ltar slob pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems/dpa' chen po ni/
7a2('bum): stong pa nyid stong pa nyid la slob pa yi/rab 'byor de ltar slob ba'i byang chub sems dpa'i sems dpa' chen po ni/chen po stong pa nyid la slob ba yin/rab 'byor de ltar sob pa'i 
7a3('bum): byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po ni/don dam pa stong pa nyid la slob ba yan/rab 'byor de ltar slo ba pa'i byang chub sems dba' sems dpa' chen po ni 'dus byas 
7a4('bum): stong pa nyid la slob pa yan/rab 'byor de ltar sleb pa'i byang chub sems dpal sems dpa' chen po ni/'dus ma byas stong pa nyid la slob pa ba yin/rab 'byor de ldar slob pa'i 
7a5('bum): byang chub sems pa dpa' sems dpa' chen bo ni/mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid la slob pa yon/rab 'byor de ltar slom pa'i byang chub sems dpas sems dpa' chen po ni

7a7('bum): 'byor de ltar slob pa'i byang chub sams dpa' sems dpa' chen po ni|rang bzhin stong pa nyid la slob payin/rab 'byor de ltar sab pa'i byang chub sems da pa' sems dpa' 
7a8('bum): chen po ni/chos thams cad stong pa nyid la slob ba yin/rab 'byor de ltar ba pa'i byang chub sems pa 'das pa'i sems dpa' chen po ni rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid la slob pa yin/

7b2('bum): ni/dngos po med pa stong pa nyid la slob yin/rab 'byor de ltar sleb pa'i byang chub sems dnga' sems dpa' chen po ni ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid la slob pa yan/rab 'byor de ltar slob
7b3('bum): pa'i byang chub sems dpa'-i sems dpa' chen po ni/dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid slob pa yin/rab 'byorade ltar slob pa'i byang chub

7b6('bum): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po ni/byang chub kyi yan lag bdun la slob pa yin/rab 'byor de ltaraslob pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chon po ni/'phags

8a1('bum): @#//rab 'byor de ltar slob pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po ni/bsam gtan bzhi la slob pa yin/rab 'byor de ltar slob ba'i byang 
8a2('bum): chub sems dpa' sems dba' chen po ni/tshad med pa bzhi la slob pa yin/rang 'byor de ltar slob pa'i byad chub sems dpa' sems pa'i chen po ni/gzugs med pa'i 
8a3('bum): snyoms par 'jug pa bzhi la slob pa yin//rab 'byor de ltar slong ba'i byang chub sems dpa' chen po ni/rnam par thar ba brgyad la slob pa yin/rab 'phyor 
8a4('bum): de ltar slob pa'i byang chub kyi sems dpa' sems dpa' sems dpa'am chen po ni/mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dgu la slob pa yin/rab 'byar de ltar slob pa'i byang 
8a5('bum): chub sems dpa'am sems dpa' chen po ni/rnam par thar ba'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa la slob pa yin/rab 'byed de ltar slob pa'i byang chub
8a6('bum): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po ni/mngon par shes pa rnams la slob pa yin/rab 'byor de ltar slob pa'i byed chub sems dpa' sems dpa'-i chen po ni/ting nge 
8a7('bum): 'dzin rnams la slob pa yin//rab tu 'byor de ltar slob pa'i byad chub sems dpa' sems pa'i chen po ni/gzungs kyi sgo rnams la slob pa yin/ba 'byor de ltar slob
8a8('bum): pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems gnas pa'i shes pa yin/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu la slob pa yin/rab 'phyor de ltar slob pa'i byang chung sems dpa' sems dpa'
8b1('bum): chen po ni/mi 'jigs pa bzhi la slob pa yin/rab 'byor de ltar slob pa'i byang chub sems dpa' chen po ni/so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi la slob pa yin//rab 'byor de ltar

9b1('bum): de ltar slob pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po ni//sems can thams cad yongs su smin par bya'o//zhes slob pa yin/rab 'byor de ltar slob pa'i byang chub

14b1('bum): thams cad 'dus par 'gyur ro//pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad dang 'thun par 'gyur ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na/rab 'byor de ni 'di ltar shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zab mo 'dir/pha rol tu phyin

20b8('bum): ldan 'das de ni 'di ltar gang nyon mongs par 'gyur ba 'am/rnam par byang bar 'gyur ba'i chos thams cad ni ma mchis shing yongs su ma mchis pa'o//de ci'i slad du zhed/
21a1('bum): @#//bcom ldan 'das de ni 'di ltar shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni shin tu dben pa'o//bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni shinatu dben pa'o//brtson 'grus kyi pha 
21a2('bum): rol tu phyin pa ni shin du dben pa'o//bzod pha'i pha rol tu phyin pa ni shinadu dben pa'o//tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni sh+tu+in tu dben pa'o//sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa ni shen
21a3('bum): dben pa'o//nang stong pa nyid ni shin tu dben pa'o//phyi stong pa nyid ni shin tu dpen pa'o//phyi nang stong pa nyid ni shin du dben pa'o//stong pa nyid stong pa nyid ni shin tu 
21a4('bum): dben pa'o//don dam pa stong pa nyid ni shin tu deben pa'o//'dus byas stong pa nyid ni shin tu baben pa'o//'dus ma byas stong pa nyid ni shinatu dben pa'o//mtha' 
21a5('bum): las 'das pa stong pa nyid ni shin du deben pa'o//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid ni shin tatu dben pa'o//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid ni shen tu dben pa'o/rang bzhin stong pa nyid ni shin
21a6('bum): tu dben pa'o//chos thams cad stong pa nyid ni shin tudaben pa'o//rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid ni shintu dben pa'o//mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid ni shinatu dpen pa'o//dngos po med pa 
21a7('bum): stong pa nyid ni shin tu dang ben pa'o//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid ni shinatu dang ben pa'o//dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid nishin tu dben pa'o//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams ni shin tu dben
21a8('bum): pa'o//yang dag par spong ba rnams ni shin tu dben pa'o//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams ni shin tu dbain pa'o//dbang po rnams ni shin tu dbenapa'o//stobs rnams ni shin tu dben ba'o//byang
21b1('bum): chub kyi yan lag rnams ni shin tu dben pa'o//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa ni shin tu dben pa'o//'phags pa'i bden pa rnams ni shin tu dben ba'o//bsam gtan rnams ni shin tu 
21b2('bum): dben pa'o//tshad med pa rnams ni shin tu dbe pa'o//gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams ni shin tu dab pa'o//rnam par thar pa rnams ni shin tu dbain pa'o//mthar gyis gnas 
21b3('bum): pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rna mas ni shin tu dben pa 'o//rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa ni shin tu dben ba'o//mngon par shes pa rnams ni shin

21b5('bum): shin tu dben pa'o//so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi ni shin tu dben pa'o//byams pa chen po ni shin tu dben pa'o//snying rje chen po ni shin tu dbe pa'o//sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa

22a8('bum): dben pa'o//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid ni shin te dben pa'o//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid shin tu dben pa'o//rang bzhin stong pa nyid ni shin tu du ban pa'o//chos thams cad stong

27a7('bum): rab 'byor byang chub sems/dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i de ltar don zab mo la spyod cing /'di lta ste nyan thos kyi sa 'am/rang sangs rgyas kyi sar 'gyur ba'i don de/
27a8('bum): mngon du mi byed pa ni dka' ba byed pa'o//gsol pa/bcom ldan 'dasakyis gsungs pa'i don bdag gis 'tshal ba ltar na/dka' bcung zad kyang mi bgyid na/
27b1('bum): ji ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dka' ba bgyid pa lags/de ci'i slad du zhe na/bcom ldan 'das de ni 'di ltar gang mngon du bgyi ba'i don de yang mi dmigs/gang 
27b2('bum): gis mngon du bgyi ba'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa de yang mi dmigs/gang mngon du bgyi ba'i chos de yang mi dmigs te bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad gang mngon du bgyis pas/

27b4('bum): zhig gis mngon du bgyi/chos mngon du bgyi ba de lta bu gang zhig mchis/bcom ldan 'das 'di ni mi dmigs pa'i spyod pa ste/da la spyod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' 
27b5('bum): chen po chos thams cad la mun pa ma mchis pa 'thob pa'o//bcom ldan 'das gal te de skad du bstan pa la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i sems mi 'jum yongs su mi 'jus zhing 
27b6('bum): mi 'jigs med dang skrag par mi 'gyur na/bcom ldan 'das de ltar spyod pa'i byang chub sems pa'i sems dpa' chen po ni/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phin pa la spyod pa ste/de ni 
27b7('bum): spyod do zhes bgyi bar yang dag par rjes su mi mthong /shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa yang dag par rjes su me mthong /bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par 
27b8('bum): rdzogs par 'tshang rgya'o zhes bgyi bar yang yang dag par rjes su mi 'thong ngo//bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' semams dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa de 
28a1('bum): @#//ni|'di snyam du bdag gis nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyis ni bsring bar bya ste/bdag ni rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du nye bar gyur to snyam yang mi 
28a2('bum): bgyid do//bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste dper na/nam mkha' ni 'di ltar bdag ni la la dang ni ring ngo//la la dang ni nye'o zhes bgyi bar mi sems so//de ci'i slad du zhe na/bcom 
28a3('bum): ldan 'das nam mkha' la bye brag ma mchis shing mi g.yo ba dang rnam par rtog pa ma mchis pa'i slad du ste/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin du          byang chub sems dpa' sems

28b1('bum): bar mi sems so/de ci'i slad du zhe na/bcom ldan 'das shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rnam par mi rtog pa'i slad du'o//bcom ldan 'das 'di lta ste dper na/gzugs

29a1('bum): @#//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams ni rnam par rtog pa thams cad sangs pa'o//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes

29a8('bum): pa ni mi rtog rnam par mi rtog go/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin du shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yang /don gang gi slad du brjod pa'i don de yang bgyid de/shes rab kyi pha

33a1('bum): @#//mnga' zhing /rnam      par rtog pa spangs par gyur to//tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu ma 'os pa'i dus na de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sang+sa

33a8('bum): las/la'u bzhi bcu dgu'o////de nas tshe dang ldan pa shA radwa ti'i bus/tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor la 'di skad ces smras so//kye mtshe dang ldan pa rab 'byorabyang chub sems
33b1('bum): dpa' sems dpa' chen po gang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa ni snying po la spyod pa'o//de skad ces smras pa dang /tsha dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis/cha dang ldan pa shA rangs ti'i bu

33b6('bum): 'dus byas stong pa nyid ni snying po me dpa'o//'dus ma byas stong pa nyid ni snyings po med pa'o//mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid ni snying ngo med pa'o//thog ma dang tha med pa stong pa nyid ni snying bo med 
33b7('bum): pa'o//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid na snying po med pa'o/rang bzhin stong pa nyid ni snying po nyid pa'o//chos thams cad stong pa nyid ni snying gi mi dpa'o//rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid ni snyings pa med pa'o//
33b8('bum): mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid na snying pa med pa'o//dngos po med pa stong pa nyid ni stong pa med pa'o//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid ni snying ba med pa'o//dngos po me dpa' ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid na snying po maid pa'o//
34a1('bum): @#//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams ni snying po med pa'o//yang dag par spong ba rnams ni snying po med pa'o //rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams ni snying po medapa'o//dbang po rnams 
34a2('bum): ni snying po med pa'o//stobs rnams ni snying po med pa'i//byang chub kyi yan lag rnams ni snying po med pa 'o//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa ni snying po med pa'o//'phags pa'i bden 
34a3('bum): pa rnams ni snying po med ba'o//bsam gtan rnams ni snying po med pa'//tshad med pa rnams ni snying po medaba'o//gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams ni snying po med:pa'o//rnam

34a7('bum): snying po med pa'o//sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams ni snying po med pa 'o//thams cad shes pa nyid ni snying po med pa'o//lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid ni snying po mid pa'o//rnam 
34a8('bum): pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid ni snying po med:pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pas ni/snying 
34b1('bum): po med pa nyid kyang mi dmigs shing yang dag par rjes su mi mthong na/snying po la dmigs pa la ga la yod/de nas 'dod pa na spyod pa dang /gzugs yod pa na spyod pa'i lha'i bu shin 
34b2('bum): tu mang ba dag 'di snyam du gyur te/rigs kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gang gis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed pa dang /gang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa 
34b3('bum): zab mo ji skad du bstan pa 'di la spyod pa dang /don 'di la spyod kyang yang dag pa'i mtha' gang mngon du byas pas/nyan thos kyis 'am rang sangs rgyas kyis la gnas par 'gyur te/yang dag 
34b4('bum): pa'i mtha' mngon du mi byed pa de dag ni phyag bya ba'i gnas te/rnam grangs des kyang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po chos kyi mnyam pa nyid 'di mngon par mi byed pa de dag ni phyag
34b5('bum): bar 'os pa'o//de nas tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis/lha'i bu de dag la 'di skad ces smras so//lha'i bu dag byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po de dag la ni chos kyi mnyam pa nyid 
34b6('bum): gang mngon du byas pas/nyin thos kyi sa'am rang sangs rgyas kyi sa la gnas par 'gyur ba de mngon du mi bya ba de ni mi dka'i/lha'i bu dag sems can tshad med grangs med dpag tu med pa thams

35a1('bum): @#//can gdul bar sems dpa' de ni/nam mkha' gdul bar sems pa dang 'dra'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/nam mkha' dben pa'i phyir/sems can dben par blta'o//nam mkha'
